- **godot**: Writes and reviews Godot 4 GDScript: scene architecture, signals, resources, state machines, save systems. - **godot**: Godot 4.7 game projects worked from files and the command line on Windows. Pins one editor version, since a newer editor rewrites project.godot and scenes and an older one downgrades them, keeps .godot/ out of git and the .uid files in it, and enforces static typing through the debug/gdscript/warnings/untyped_declaration setting. Edits .tscn and .tres by hand: section order, ext_resource and sub_resource ids, uid:// references, parent paths and [connection] lines. Connects and emits signals and registers autoloads.
